%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\\n    \\\"libero_spatial\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_object\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_goal\\\": 800,\\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Pruning hook\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float, mode: str = \\\"l2_norm\\\"):\\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\\n        log.info(\\\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\\\", keep_ratio)\\n        return\\n\\n    original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\\n\\n    @wraps(original_encode)\\n    def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\\n        features = original_encode(pixel_values)\\n        N = features.shape[1]\\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\\n        if k >= N:\\n            return features\\n        if mode == \\\"l2_norm\\\":\\n            norms = features.norm(dim=-1)\\n for _ in range(features.shape[0])\\n            ], dim=0)\\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\\n        pruned = features.gather(\\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\\n        )\\n        return pruned\\n\\n    model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\\n    log.info(\\\"Vision pruning installed: time_mod.time() - eval_start\\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    log.info(\\\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: xvla | keep_ratio: %.2f\\\", args.suite, args.keep_ratio)\\n    log.info(\\\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\\\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\\n    log.info(\\\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\\\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\\n        log.info(\\\" OpenVLA (openvla-7b) is based on Prismatic VLM architecture - it has a vision encoder (SigLIP + DinoV2) that produces image features, which are projected into the LLM's (Llama 2 7B) embedding space. The model generates action tokens autoregressively.\n\n\nNow I'm tracing through the forward pass to find where to intercept the vision tokens. \\\"---\\\\nlibrary_name: transformers\\\\ntags:\\\\n- robotics\\\\n- vla\\\\n- image-text-to-text\\\\n- multimodal\\\\n- pretraining\\\\nlicense: mit\\\\nlanguage:\\\\n- en\\\\npipeline_tag: image-text-to-text\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n# OpenVLA 7B Fine-Tuned on LIBERO-Object\\\\n\\\\nThis model was produced by fine-tuning the [OpenVLA 7B model](https://huggingface.co/openvla/openvla-7b) via\\\\nLoRA (r=32) on the LIBERO-Object dataset from the [LIBERO simulation benchmark](https://libero-project.github.io/main.html).\\\\nWe made a few modifications to the training dataset to improve final performance (see the\\\\n[OpenVLA paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09246) for details).\\\\n\\\\nBelow are the hyperparameters we used for all LIBERO experiments:\\\\n\\\\n- Hardware: 8 x A100 GPUs with 80GB memory\\\\n- Fine-tuned with LoRA: `use_lora == True`, `lora_rank == 32`, `lora_dropout == 0.0`\\\\n- Learning rate: 5e-4\\\\n- Batch size: 128 (8 GPUs x 16 samples each)\\\\n- Number of training gradient steps: 50K\\\\n- No quantization at train or test time\\\\n- No gradient accumulation (i.e. `grad_accumulation_steps == 1`)\\\\n- `shuffle_buffer_size == 100_000`\\\\n- Image augmentations: Random crop, color jitter (see training code for details)\\\\n\\\\n## Usage Instructions\\\\n\\\\nSee the [OpenVLA GitHub README](https://github.com/openvla/openvla/blob/main/README.md) for instructions on how to\\\\nrun and evaluate this model in the LIBERO simulator.\\\\n\\\\n## Citation\\\\n\\\\n**BibTeX:**\\\\n\\\\n```bibtex\\\\n@article{kim24openvla,\\\\n    title={OpenVLA: An \"---\\nlibrary_name: transformers\\ntags:\\n- robotics\\n- vla\\n- image-text-to-text\\n- multimodal\\n- pretraining\\nlicense: mit\\nlanguage:\\n- en\\npipeline_tag: image-text-to-text\\n---\\n\\n# OpenVLA 7B Fine-Tuned on LIBERO-Object\\n\\nThis model was produced by fine-tuning the [OpenVLA 7B model](https://huggingface.co/openvla/openvla-7b) via\\nLoRA (r=32) on the LIBERO-Object dataset from the [LIBERO simulation benchmark](https://libero-project.github.io/main.html).\\nWe made a few modifications to the training dataset to improve final performance (see the\\n[OpenVLA paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09246) for details).\\n\\nBelow are the hyperparameters we used for all LIBERO experiments:\\n\\n- Hardware: 8 x A100 GPUs with 80GB memory\\n- Fine-tuned with LoRA: `use_lora == True`, `lora_rank == 32`, `lora_dropout == 0.0`\\n- Learning rate: 5e-4\\n- Batch size: 128 (8 GPUs x 16 samples each)\\n- Number of training gradient steps: 50K\\n- No quantization at train or test time\\n- No gradient accumulation (i.e. `grad_accumulation_steps == 1`)\\n- `shuffle_buffer_size == 100_000`\\n- Image augmentations: Random crop, color jitter (see training code for details)\\n\\n## Usage Instructions\\n\\nSee the [OpenVLA GitHub README](https://github.com/openvla/openvla/blob/main/README.md) for instructions on how to\\nrun and evaluate this model in the LIBERO {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01PXXiyA2dKtd1NrhLvP3FWT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I understand the OpenVLA architecture:\n\n1. `vision_backbone` (PrismaticVisionBackbone): SigLIP + DinoV2 → produces patch features\n2. `projector` (PrismaticProjector): Projects patch features to LLM dim → projected_patch_embeddings\n3. The projected_patch_embeddings are concatenated with text embeddings and fed to the LLM\n\nFor L2 norm pruning, I need to intercept after the projector (step 2) and before concatenation (step 3). The pruning hook should:\n1. Let forward run normally through vision_backbone and projector\n\n\n2. After projector output, prune by L2 norm\n3. Feed the pruned tokens to the LLM\n\nLooking at the forward method, the flow is: vision_backbone processes pixel values to get patch features, then the projector transforms those to the LLM dimension, and finally they're concatenated with text embeddings. The simplest approach is to wrap the projector so it prunes the embeddings right after projection completes.\n\nLet me trace through the rest of the forward method to see exactly how the projected embeddings flow through the OpenVLAForActionPrediction(PrismaticForConditionalGeneration):\\n    config_class: PretrainedConfig = OpenVLAConfig\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: OpenVLAConfig) -> None:\\n        super().__init__(config)\\n        self.norm_stats = config.norm_stats\\n\\n        # Compute action bins\\n        self.bins = np.linspace(-1, 1, config.n_action_bins)\\n        self.bin_centers = (self.bins[:-1] + self.bins[1:]) / 2.0\\n\\n        # Compute vocab size for de-tokenization -- revert added \\\"multiple of\\\"\\n        self.vocab_size = self.config.text_config.vocab_size - self.config.pad_to_multiple_of\\n\\n    def predict_action(\\n        self, input_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None, unnorm_key: Optional[str] = None, **kwargs: str\\n    ) -> np.ndarray:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Thin wrapper around .generate() that decodes predicted actions and unnormalizes them.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # If the special empty token ('') does not already appear after the colon (':') token in the prompt\\n        # (after \\\"OUT:\\\" or \\\"ASSISTANT:\\\"), insert it to match the inputs seen at training time\\n        if not torch.all(input_ids[:, -1] == 29871):\\n            input_ids = torch.cat(\\n                (input_ids, torch.unsqueeze(torch.Tensor([29871]).long(), dim=0).to(input_ids.device)), dim=1\\n            )\\n\\n        # Run VLA inference\\n        generated_ids = self.generate(input_ids, max_new_tokens=self.get_action_dim(unnorm_key), **kwargs)\\n\\n        # Extract predicted action tokens and translate into (normalized) continuous actions\\n discretized_actions = np.clip(discretized_actions - 1, a_min=0, a_max=self.bin_centers.shape[0] - 1)\\n        normalized_actions = self.bin_centers[discretized_actions]\\n\\n        # Unnormalize actions\\n        action_norm_stats = self.get_action_stats(unnorm_key)\\n        mask = action_norm_stats.get(\\\"mask\\\", np.ones_like(action_norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"], dtype=bool))\\n        action_high, action_low = np.array(action_norm_stats[\\\"q99\\\"]), np.array(action_norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"])\\n        actions = np.where(\\n            mask,\\n            0.5 * (normalized_actions + 1) * (action_high - action_low) + action_low,\\n            normalized_actions,\\n        )\\n\\n        return actions\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _check_unnorm_key(norm_stats: Dict[str, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_013AhnSTBngYVd4rZTKjTMst","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013a49xkzUimqSVyDuqGSqFz","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/eval_openvla_pruning_libero.py","content":"\"\"\"OpenVLA Vision Token Pruning Evaluation on LIBERO.\n\nPrunes vision tokens by L2 norm after the projector (before LLM),\nevaluates on LIBERO suites using the official OpenVLA action prediction API.\n\nUsage:\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 MUJOCO_GL=egl python eval_openvla_pruning_libero.py \\\n        --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-spatial \\\n        --suite libero_spatial \\\n        --keep_ratio 0.9 \\\n        --num_episodes 50 \\\n        --gpu 0\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time as time_mod\nfrom functools import wraps\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"MUJOCO_GL\", \"egl\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom PIL import Image\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\n    \"libero_spatial\": 280,\n    \"libero_object\": 280,\n    \"libero_goal\": 300,\n    \"libero_10\": 520,\n    \"libero_90\": 400,\n}\n\nOPENVLA_PROMPT_TEMPLATE = \"In: What action should the robot take to {task}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"OpenVLA vision token pruning eval on LIBERO\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--model_path\", type=str, required=True,\n                   help=\"Path to fine-tuned OpenVLA model directory\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_object\",\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\n    p.add_argument(\"--keep_ratio\", type=float, default=1.0,\n                   help=\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=50)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_ids\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--mode\", type=str, default=\"l2_norm\",\n                   choices=[\"l2_norm\", \"random\"],\n                   help=\"Pruning method\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Pruning hook — patches the projector to prune after projection\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float, mode: str = \"l2_norm\"):\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\", keep_ratio)\n        return 0, 0\n\n    original_forward = model.projector.forward\n\n    @wraps(original_forward)\n    def pruned_forward(img_patches):\n        projected = original_forward(img_patches)\n        B, N, D = projected.shape\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n        if k >= N:\n            return projected\n        if mode == \"l2_norm\":\n            norms = projected.norm(dim=-1)\n            _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n        elif mode == \"random\":\n            indices = torch.stack([\n                torch.randperm(N, device=projected.device)[:k]\n                for _ in range(B)\n            ], dim=0)\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n        pruned = projected.gather(\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D)\n        )\n        return pruned\n\n    model.projector.forward = pruned_forward\n\n    dummy_input = torch.randn(1, 256, model.projector.projector[0].in_features,\n                              device=next(model.parameters()).device,\n                              dtype=next(model.parameters()).dtype)\n    test_out = original_forward(dummy_input)\n    n_tokens = test_out.shape[1]\n    k_tokens = max(1, int(keep_ratio * n_tokens))\n\n    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f, mode=%s, %d→%d tokens\",\n             keep_ratio, mode, n_tokens, k_tokens)\n    return n_tokens, k_tokens\n\n\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\n\n    log.info(\"Loading OpenVLA from %s\", model_path)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        model_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    ).to(device)\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model.eval()\n\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    log.info(\"Model loaded: %.1fB params, dtype=%s\", n_params / 1e9, next(model.parameters()).dtype)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef wilson_ci(successes, total, z=1.96):\n    if total == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    p_hat = successes / total\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / total\n    center = (p_hat + z**2 / (2 * total)) / denom\n    margin = z * np.sqrt((p_hat * (1 - p_hat) + z**2 / (4 * total)) / total) / denom\n    return center, max(0, center - margin), min(1, center + margin)\n\n\ndef get_unnorm_key(model, suite: str) -> str:\n    norm_stats = model.norm_stats\n    suite_to_key = {\n        \"libero_spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n        \"libero_object\": \"libero_object\",\n        \"libero_goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n        \"libero_10\": \"libero_10\",\n        \"libero_90\": \"libero_90\",\n    }\n    key = suite_to_key.get(suite, suite)\n    if key in norm_stats:\n        return key\n    if len(norm_stats) == 1:\n        only_key = next(iter(norm_stats.keys()))\n        log.warning(\"Suite '%s' not in norm_stats, using '%s'\", suite, only_key)\n        return only_key\n    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find norm_stats for suite '{suite}', available: {list(norm_stats.keys())}\")\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_action(model, processor, image: Image.Image, task_text: str,\n                   unnorm_key: str, device: torch.device) -> np.ndarray:\n    prompt = OPENVLA_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(task=task_text)\n    inputs = processor(prompt, image).to(device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    action = model.predict_action(\n        **inputs,\n        unnorm_key=unnorm_key,\n        do_sample=False,\n    )\n    return action\n\n\ndef run_evaluation(args):\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n    log.info(\"Device: %s\", device)\n\n    model, processor = load_model(args.model_path, device)\n    unnorm_key = get_unnorm_key(model, args.suite)\n    log.info(\"Using unnorm_key='%s' for suite '%s'\", unnorm_key, args.suite)\n\n    n_vis_tokens, k_tokens = install_vision_pruning(model, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 300)\n\n    output_path = Path(args.output) if args.output else Path(\n        f\"eval_results/openvla_{args.mode}_k{args.keep_ratio}_{args.suite}_seed{args.seed}.json\")\n\n    log.info(\"Suite: %s | Device: %s | keep_ratio=%.2f | Episodes/task: %d | Max steps: %d\",\n             args.suite, device, args.keep_ratio, args.num_episodes, max_steps)\n\n    # Load LIBERO benchmark\n    from lerobot.common.envs.factory import make_env_from_cfg\n    from libero.libero.benchmark import get_benchmark\n    benchmark = get_benchmark(args.suite)()\n    n_tasks = benchmark.n_tasks\n    task_ids = list(range(n_tasks))\n    if args.task_ids:\n        task_ids = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\",\")]\n    log.info(\"Tasks: %d total, evaluating: %s\", n_tasks, task_ids)\n\n    from lerobot.common.envs.libero.env import LiberoEnv\n\n    eval_start = time_mod.time()\n    total_successes = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n    all_task_results = {}\n\n    for task_id in task_ids:\n        task_suite = benchmark\n        task = benchmark.get_task(task_id)\n        task_desc = task.language\n        log.info(\"--- Task %d: %s ---\", task_id, task_desc)\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=task_suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=224,\n            observation_height=224,\n            init_states=True,\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\n            episode_index=0,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n        )\n\n        task_successes = []\n        task_lengths = []\n\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if step < 3 or step % 50 == 0:\n                    log.info(\"  Step %d/%d\", step, max_steps)\n\n                # Extract image from observation\n                img = obs.get(\"agentview_image\", obs.get(\"pixels\", {}).get(\"agentview_image\", None))\n                if img is None:\n                    for k, v in obs.items():\n                        if \"agentview\" in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\n                            img = v\n                            break\n                if img is None:\n                    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find agentview image in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\")\n\n                if isinstance(img, np.ndarray):\n                    if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\n                        img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\n                    pil_img = Image.fromarray(img)\n                else:\n                    pil_img = img\n\n                action = predict_action(model, processor, pil_img, task_desc, unnorm_key, device)\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n        env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\n\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": n_total,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += n_total\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\n\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: openvla | keep_ratio: %.2f | mode: %s\",\n             args.suite, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n    log.info(\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\n    log.info(\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\n        log.info(\"  Task %d: %.0f%% (%d/%d) -- %s\",\n                 tid, tr[\"success_rate\"] * 100, tr[\"success_count\"],\n                 tr[\"total_episodes\"], tr[\"task_description\"])\n    log.info(\"Elapsed: %.1f min\", elapsed / 60)\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"openvla\",\n        \"model_path\": args.model_path,\n        \"mode\": args.mode,\n        \"suite\": args.suite,\n        \"keep_ratio\": args.keep_ratio,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_original\": n_vis_tokens,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_kept\": k_tokens,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"total_successes\": total_successes,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": round(overall_sr, 4),\n        \"wilson_ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"num_episodes_per_task\": args.num_episodes,\n        \"max_steps\": max_steps,\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"per_task\": all_task_results,\n    }\n\n    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Results saved to %s\", output_path)\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    args = parse_args()\n    run_evaluation(args)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1152,"cache_read_input_tokens":59739,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1152},"output_tokens":26,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"37b1b726-ff96-4520-a9d5-f75b8f842f87","uuid":"a9f6ff9a-0f18-40b3-8db0-5c83c19e9da5"}

on LIBERO\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--model_path\", type=str, required=True,\n                   help=\"Path to fine-tuned OpenVLA model directory\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_object\",\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\n    p.add_argument(\"--keep_ratio\", type=float, default=1.0,\n                   help=\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=50)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_ids\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--mode\", type=str, default=\"l2_norm\",\n                   choices=[\"l2_norm\", \"random\"],\n                   help=\"Pruning method\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Pruning hook — patches the projector to prune after projection\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float, mode: str = \"l2_norm\"):\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\", keep_ratio)\n        return 0, 0\n\n    original_forward = model.projector.forward\n\n    @wraps(original_forward)\n    def pruned_forward(img_patches):\n        projected = original_forward(img_patches)\n        B, N, D = projected.shape\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n        if k >= N:\n            return projected\n        if mode == \"l2_norm\":\n            norms = projected.norm(dim=-1)\n            _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n        elif mode == \"random\":\n            indices = torch.stack([\n                torch.randperm(N, device=projected.device)[:k]\n                for _ in range(B)\n            ], dim=0)\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n        pruned = projected.gather(\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D)\n        )\n        return pruned\n\n    model.projector.forward = pruned_forward\n\n    dummy_input = torch.randn(1, 256, model.projector.projector[0].in_features,\n                              device=next(model.parameters()).device,\n                              dtype=next(model.parameters()).dtype)\n    test_out = original_forward(dummy_input)\n    n_tokens = test_out.shape[1]\n    k_tokens = max(1, int(keep_ratio * n_tokens))\n\n    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f, mode=%s, %d→%d tokens\",\n             keep_ratio, mode, n_tokens, k_tokens)\n    return n_tokens, k_tokens\n\n\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\n\n    log.info(\"Loading OpenVLA from %s\", model_path)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        model_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    ).to(device)\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model.eval()\n\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    log.info(\"Model loaded: %.1fB params, dtype=%s\", n_params / 1e9, next(model.parameters()).dtype)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef wilson_ci(successes, total, z=1.96):\n    if total == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    p_hat = successes / total\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / total\n    center = (p_hat + z**2 / (2 * total)) / denom\n    margin = z * np.sqrt((p_hat * (1 - p_hat) + z**2 / (4 * total)) / total) / denom\n    return center, max(0, center - margin), min(1, center + margin)\n\n\ndef get_unnorm_key(model, suite: str) -> str:\n    norm_stats = model.norm_stats\n    suite_to_key = {\n        \"libero_spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n        \"libero_object\": \"libero_object\",\n        \"libero_goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n        \"libero_10\": \"libero_10\",\n        \"libero_90\": \"libero_90\",\n    }\n    key = suite_to_key.get(suite, suite)\n    if key in norm_stats:\n        return key\n    if len(norm_stats) == 1:\n        only_key = next(iter(norm_stats.keys()))\n        log.warning(\"Suite '%s' not in norm_stats, using '%s'\", suite, only_key)\n        return only_key\n    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find norm_stats for suite '{suite}', available: {list(norm_stats.keys())}\")\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_action(model, processor, image: Image.Image, task_text: str,\n                   unnorm_key: str, device: torch.device) -> np.ndarray:\n    prompt = OPENVLA_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(task=task_text)\n    inputs = processor(prompt, image).to(device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    action = model.predict_action(\n        **inputs,\n        unnorm_key=unnorm_key,\n        do_sample=False,\n    )\n    return action\n\n\ndef run_evaluation(args):\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n    log.info(\"Device: %s\", device)\n\n    model, processor = load_model(args.model_path, device)\n    unnorm_key = get_unnorm_key(model, args.suite)\n    log.info(\"Using unnorm_key='%s' for suite '%s'\", unnorm_key, args.suite)\n\n    n_vis_tokens, k_tokens = install_vision_pruning(model, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 300)\n\n    output_path = Path(args.output) if args.output else Path(\n        f\"eval_results/openvla_{args.mode}_k{args.keep_ratio}_{args.suite}_seed{args.seed}.json\")\n\n    log.info(\"Suite: %s | Device: %s | keep_ratio=%.2f | Episodes/task: %d | Max steps: %d\",\n             args.suite, device, args.keep_ratio, args.num_episodes, max_steps)\n\n    # Load LIBERO benchmark\n    from lerobot.common.envs.factory import make_env_from_cfg\n    from libero.libero.benchmark import get_benchmark\n    benchmark = get_benchmark(args.suite)()\n    n_tasks = benchmark.n_tasks\n    task_ids = list(range(n_tasks))\n    if args.task_ids:\n        task_ids = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\",\")]\n    log.info(\"Tasks: %d total, evaluating: %s\", n_tasks, task_ids)\n\n    from lerobot.common.envs.libero.env import LiberoEnv\n\n    eval_start = time_mod.time()\n    total_successes = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n    all_task_results = {}\n\n    for task_id in task_ids:\n        task_suite = benchmark\n        task = benchmark.get_task(task_id)\n        task_desc = task.language\n        log.info(\"--- Task %d: %s ---\", task_id, task_desc)\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=task_suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=224,\n            observation_height=224,\n            init_states=True,\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\n            episode_index=0,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n        )\n\n        task_successes = []\n        task_lengths = []\n\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if step < 3 or step % 50 == 0:\n                    log.info(\"  Step %d/%d\", step, max_steps)\n\n                # Extract image from observation\n                img = obs.get(\"agentview_image\", obs.get(\"pixels\", {}).get(\"agentview_image\", None))\n                if img is None:\n                    for k, v in obs.items():\n                        if \"agentview\" in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\n                            img = v\n                            break\n                if img is None:\n                    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find agentview image in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\")\n\n                if isinstance(img, np.ndarray):\n                    if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\n                        img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\n                    pil_img = Image.fromarray(img)\n                else:\n                    pil_img = img\n\n                action = predict_action(model, processor, pil_img, task_desc, unnorm_key, device)\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n        env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\n\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": n_total,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += n_total\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\n\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: openvla | keep_ratio: %.2f | mode: %s\",\n             args.suite, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n    log.info(\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\n    log.info(\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\n        log.info(\"  Task %d: %.0f%% (%d/%d) -- %s\",\n                 tid, tr[\"success_rate\"] * 100, tr[\"success_count\"],\n                 tr[\"total_episodes\"], tr[\"task_description\"])\n    log.info(\"Elapsed: %.1f min\", elapsed / 60)\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"openvla\",\n        \"model_path\": args.model_path,\n        \"mode\": args.mode,\n        \"suite\": args.suite,\n        \"keep_ratio\": args.keep_ratio,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_original\": n_vis_tokens,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_kept\": k_tokens,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"total_successes\": total_successes,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": round(overall_sr, 4),\n        \"wilson_ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"num_episodes_per_task\": args.num_episodes,\n        \"max_steps\": max_steps,\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"per_task\": all_task_results,\n    }\n\n    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Results saved to %s\", output_path)\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    args = parse_args()\n    run_evaluation(args)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

                  help=\"Pruning method\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Pruning hook — patches the projector to prune after projection\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float, mode: str = \"l2_norm\"):\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\", keep_ratio)\n        return 0, 0\n\n    original_forward = model.projector.forward\n\n    @wraps(original_forward)\n    def pruned_forward(img_patches):\n        projected = original_forward(img_patches)\n        B, N, D = projected.shape\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n        if k >= N:\n            return projected\n        if mode == \"l2_norm\":\n            norms = projected.norm(dim=-1)\n            _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n        elif mode == \"random\":\n            indices = torch.stack([\n                torch.randperm(N, device=projected.device)[:k]\n                for _ in range(B)\n            ], dim=0)\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n        pruned = projected.gather(\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D)\n        )\n        return pruned\n\n    model.projector.forward = pruned_forward\n\n    dummy_input = original_forward = model.projector.forward\n\n    @wraps(original_forward)\n    def pruned_forward(img_patches):\n        projected = original_forward(img_patches)\n        B, N, D = projected.shape\n n_tokens, k_tokens","new_string":"def get_n_vision_tokens(model):\n    img_sizes = model.config.image_sizes\n    patch_size = 14\n    n_patches = (img_sizes[0] // patch_size) * (img_sizes[1] // patch_size)\n    return n_patches\n\n\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float, mode: str = \"l2_norm\"):\n    n_tokens = get_n_vision_tokens(model)\n    k_tokens = max(1, int(keep_ratio * n_tokens))\n\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied (%d tokens)\", keep_ratio, n_tokens)\n        return n_tokens, n_tokens\n\n    original_forward = model.projector.forward\n\n    @wraps(original_forward)\n    def pruned_forward(img_patches):\n        projected = original_forward(img_patches)\n        B, N, D = projected.shape\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n        if k >= N:\n            return projected\n        if mode == \"l2_norm\":\n            norms = projected.norm(dim=-1)\n            _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n model.projector.forward = pruned_forward\n\n    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f, mode=%s, %d→%d tokens\",\n             keep_ratio, mode, n_tokens, k_tokens)\n    return n_tokens, k_tokens","originalFile":"\"\"\"OpenVLA Vision Token Pruning Evaluation on LIBERO.\n\nPrunes vision tokens by L2 norm after the projector (before LLM),\nevaluates on LIBERO suites using the official OpenVLA action prediction API.\n\nUsage:\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 MUJOCO_GL=egl python eval_openvla_pruning_libero.py \\\n        --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-spatial \\\n        --suite libero_spatial \\\n        --keep_ratio 0.9 \\\n        --num_episodes 50 \\\n        --gpu 0\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time as time_mod\nfrom functools import wraps\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"MUJOCO_GL\", \"egl\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom PIL import Image\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\n    \"libero_spatial\": 280,\n    \"libero_object\": 280,\n    \"libero_goal\": 300,\n    \"libero_10\": 520,\n    \"libero_90\": 400,\n}\n\nOPENVLA_PROMPT_TEMPLATE = \"In: What action should the robot take to {task}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"OpenVLA vision token pruning eval on LIBERO\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--model_path\", type=str, required=True,\n                   help=\"Path to fine-tuned OpenVLA model directory\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_object\",\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\n    p.add_argument(\"--keep_ratio\", type=float, default=1.0,\n                   help=\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=50)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_ids\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--mode\", type=str, default=\"l2_norm\",\n                   choices=[\"l2_norm\", \"random\"],\n                   help=\"Pruning method\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Pruning hook — patches the projector to prune after projection\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float, mode: str = \"l2_norm\"):\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\", keep_ratio)\n        return 0, 0\n\n    original_forward = model.projector.forward\n\n    @wraps(original_forward)\n    def pruned_forward(img_patches):\n        projected = original_forward(img_patches)\n        B, N, D = projected.shape\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n        if k >= N:\n            return projected\n        if mode == \"l2_norm\":\n            norms = projected.norm(dim=-1)\n            _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n        elif mode == \"random\":\n            indices = torch.stack([\n                torch.randperm(N, device=projected.device)[:k]\n                for _ in range(B)\n            ], dim=0)\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n        pruned = projected.gather(\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D)\n        )\n        return pruned\n\n    model.projector.forward = pruned_forward\n\n    dummy_input = torch.randn(1, 256, model.projector.projector[0].in_features,\n                              device=next(model.parameters()).device,\n                              dtype=next(model.parameters()).dtype)\n    test_out = original_forward(dummy_input)\n    n_tokens = test_out.shape[1]\n    k_tokens = max(1, int(keep_ratio * n_tokens))\n\n    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f, mode=%s, %d→%d tokens\",\n             keep_ratio, mode, n_tokens, k_tokens)\n    return n_tokens, k_tokens\n\n\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\n\n    log.info(\"Loading OpenVLA from %s\", model_path)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        model_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    ).to(device)\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model.eval()\n\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    log.info(\"Model loaded: %.1fB params, dtype=%s\", n_params / 1e9, next(model.parameters()).dtype)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef wilson_ci(successes, total, z=1.96):\n    if total == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    p_hat = successes / total\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / total\n    center = (p_hat + z**2 / (2 * total)) / denom\n    margin = z * np.sqrt((p_hat * (1 - p_hat) + z**2 / (4 * total)) / total) / denom\n    return center, max(0, center - margin), min(1, center + margin)\n\n\ndef get_unnorm_key(model, suite: str) -> str:\n    norm_stats = model.norm_stats\n    suite_to_key = {\n        \"libero_spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n        \"libero_object\": \"libero_object\",\n        \"libero_goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n        \"libero_10\": \"libero_10\",\n        \"libero_90\": \"libero_90\",\n    }\n    key = suite_to_key.get(suite, suite)\n    if key in norm_stats:\n        return key\n    if len(norm_stats) == 1:\n        only_key = next(iter(norm_stats.keys()))\n        log.warning(\"Suite '%s' not in norm_stats, using '%s'\", suite, only_key)\n        return only_key\n    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find norm_stats for suite '{suite}', available: {list(norm_stats.keys())}\")\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_action(model, processor, image: Image.Image, task_text: str,\n                   unnorm_key: str, device: torch.device) -> np.ndarray:\n    prompt = OPENVLA_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(task=task_text)\n    inputs = processor(prompt, image).to(device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    action = model.predict_action(\n        **inputs,\n        unnorm_key=unnorm_key,\n        do_sample=False,\n    )\n    return action\n\n\ndef run_evaluation(args):\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n    log.info(\"Device: %s\", device)\n\n    model, processor = load_model(args.model_path, device)\n    unnorm_key = get_unnorm_key(model, args.suite)\n    log.info(\"Using unnorm_key='%s' for suite '%s'\", unnorm_key, args.suite)\n\n    n_vis_tokens, k_tokens = install_vision_pruning(model, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 300)\n\n    output_path = Path(args.output) if args.output else Path(\n        f\"eval_results/openvla_{args.mode}_k{args.keep_ratio}_{args.suite}_seed{args.seed}.json\")\n\n    log.info(\"Suite: %s | Device: %s | keep_ratio=%.2f | Episodes/task: %d | Max steps: %d\",\n             args.suite, device, args.keep_ratio, args.num_episodes, max_steps)\n\n    # Load LIBERO benchmark\n    from lerobot.common.envs.factory import make_env_from_cfg\n    from libero.libero.benchmark import get_benchmark\n    benchmark = get_benchmark(args.suite)()\n    n_tasks = benchmark.n_tasks\n    task_ids = list(range(n_tasks))\n    if args.task_ids:\n        task_ids = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\",\")]\n    log.info(\"Tasks: %d total, evaluating: %s\", n_tasks, task_ids)\n\n    from lerobot.common.envs.libero.env import LiberoEnv\n\n    eval_start = time_mod.time()\n    total_successes = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n    all_task_results = {}\n\n    for task_id in task_ids:\n        task_suite = benchmark\n        task = benchmark.get_task(task_id)\n        task_desc = task.language\n        log.info(\"--- Task %d: %s ---\", task_id, task_desc)\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=task_suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=224,\n            observation_height=224,\n            init_states=True,\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\n            episode_index=0,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n        )\n\n        task_successes = []\n        task_lengths = []\n\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if step < 3 or step % 50 == 0:\n                    log.info(\"  Step %d/%d\", step, max_steps)\n\n                # Extract image from observation\n                img = obs.get(\"agentview_image\", obs.get(\"pixels\", {}).get(\"agentview_image\", None))\n                if img is None:\n                    for k, v in obs.items():\n                        if \"agentview\" in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\n                            img = v\n                            break\n                if img is None:\n                    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find agentview image in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\")\n\n                if isinstance(img, np.ndarray):\n                    if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\n                        img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\n                    pil_img = Image.fromarray(img)\n                else:\n                    pil_img = img\n\n                action = predict_action(model, processor, pil_img, task_desc, unnorm_key, device)\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n        env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\n\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": n_total,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += n_total\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\n\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: openvla | keep_ratio: %.2f | mode: %s\",\n             args.suite, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n    log.info(\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\n    log.info(\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\n        log.info(\"  Task %d: %.0f%% (%d/%d) -- %s\",\n                 tid, tr[\"success_rate\"] * 100, tr[\"success_count\"],\n                 tr[\"total_episodes\"], tr[\"task_description\"])\n    log.info(\"Elapsed: %.1f min\", elapsed / 60)\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"openvla\",\n        \"model_path\": args.model_path,\n        \"mode\": args.mode,\n        \"suite\": args.suite,\n        \"keep_ratio\": args.keep_ratio,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_original\": n_vis_tokens,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_kept\": k_tokens,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"total_successes\": total_successes,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": round(overall_sr, 4),\n        \"wilson_ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"num_episodes_per_task\": args.num_episodes,\n        \"max_steps\": max_steps,\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"per_task\": all_task_results,\n    }\n\n    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Results saved to %s\", output_path)\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    args = parse_args()\n    run_evaluation(args)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":75,"oldLines":10,"newStart":75,"newLines":20,"lines":[" # Pruning hook — patches the projector to prune after projection"," # indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n        pruned = projected.gather(\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D)\n        )\n        return pruned\n\n    model.projector.forward = pruned_forward\n\n    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f, mode=%s, %d->%d tokens\",\n             keep_ratio, mode, n_tokens, k_tokens)\n    return n_tokens, k_tokens\n\n\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\n\n    log.info(\"Loading OpenVLA from %s\", model_path)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        model_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    ).to(device)\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model.eval()\n\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    log.info(\"Model loaded: %.1fB params, dtype=%s\", n_params / 1e9, next(model.parameters()).dtype)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef wilson_ci(successes, total, z=1.96):\n    if total == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    p_hat = successes / total\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / total\n    center = (p_hat + z**2 / (2 * total)) / denom\n    margin = z * np.sqrt((p_hat * (1 - p_hat) + z**2 / (4 * total)) / total) / denom\n    return center, max(0, center - margin), min(1, center + margin)\n\n\ndef get_unnorm_key(model, suite: str) -> str:\n    norm_stats = model.norm_stats\n    suite_to_key = {\n        \"libero_spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n        \"libero_object\": \"libero_object\",\n        \"libero_goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n        \"libero_10\": \"libero_10\",\n        \"libero_90\": \"libero_90\",\n    }\n    key = suite_to_key.get(suite, suite)\n    if key in norm_stats:\n        return key\n    if len(norm_stats) == 1:\n        only_key = next(iter(norm_stats.keys()))\n        log.warning(\"Suite '%s' not in norm_stats, using '%s'\", suite, only_key)\n        return only_key\n    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find norm_stats for suite '{suite}', available: {list(norm_stats.keys())}\")\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_action(model, processor, image: Image.Image, task_text: str,\n                   unnorm_key: str, device: torch.device) -> np.ndarray:\n    prompt = OPENVLA_PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(task=task_text)\n    inputs = processor(prompt, image).to(device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    action = model.predict_action(\n        **inputs,\n        unnorm_key=unnorm_key,\n        do_sample=False,\n    )\n    return action\n\n\ndef run_evaluation(args):\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n    log.info(\"Device: %s\", device)\n\n    model, processor = load_model(args.model_path, device)\n    unnorm_key = get_unnorm_key(model, args.suite)\n    log.info(\"Using unnorm_key='%s' for suite '%s'\", unnorm_key, args.suite)\n\n    n_vis_tokens, k_tokens = install_vision_pruning(model, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 300)\n\n    output_path = Path(args.output) if args.output else Path(\n        f\"eval_results/openvla_{args.mode}_k{args.keep_ratio}_{args.suite}_seed{args.seed}.json\")\n\n    log.info(\"Suite: %s | Device: %s | keep_ratio=%.2f | Episodes/task: %d | Max steps: %d\",\n             args.suite, device, args.keep_ratio, args.num_episodes, max_steps)\n\n    # Load LIBERO benchmark\n    from libero.libero.benchmark import get_benchmark\n    benchmark = get_benchmark(args.suite)()\n    n_tasks = benchmark.n_tasks\n    task_ids = list(range(n_tasks))\n    if args.task_ids:\n        task_ids = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\",\")]\n    log.info(\"Tasks: %d total, evaluating: %s\", n_tasks, task_ids)\n\n    from lerobot.common.envs.libero.env import LiberoEnv\n\n    eval_start = time_mod.time()\n    total_successes = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n    all_task_results = {}\n\n    for task_id in task_ids:\n        task_suite = benchmark\n        task = benchmark.get_task(task_id)\n        task_desc = task.language\n        log.info(\"--- Task %d: %s ---\", task_id, task_desc)\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=task_suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=224,\n            observation_height=224,\n            init_states=True,\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\n            episode_index=0,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n        )\n\n        task_successes = []\n        task_lengths = []\n\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if step < 3 or step % 50 == 0:\n                    log.info(\"  Step %d/%d\", step, max_steps)\n\n                img = obs.get(\"agentview_image\", obs.get(\"pixels\", {}).get(\"agentview_image\", None))\n                if img is None:\n                    for k, v in obs.items():\n                        if \"agentview\" in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\n                            img = v\n                            break\n                if img is None:\n                    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot find agentview image in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\")\n\n                if isinstance(img, np.ndarray):\n                    if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\n                        img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\n                    pil_img = Image.fromarray(img)\n                else:\n                    pil_img = img\n\n                action = predict_action(model, processor, pil_img, task_desc, unnorm_key, device)\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n        env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\n\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": n_total,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += n_total\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\n\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: openvla | keep_ratio: %.2f | mode: %s\",\n             args.suite, args.keep_ratio, args.mode)\n    log.info(\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\n    log.info(\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\n        log.info(\"  Task %d: %.0f%% (%d/%d) -- %s\",\n                 tid, tr[\"success_rate\"] * 100, tr[\"success_count\"],\n                 tr[\"total_episodes\"], tr[\"task_description\"])\n    log.info(\"Elapsed: %.1f min\", elapsed / 60)\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"openvla\",\n        \"model_path\": args.model_path,\n        \"mode\": args.mode,\n        \"suite\": args.suite,\n        \"keep_ratio\": args.keep_ratio,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_original\": n_vis_tokens,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_kept\": k_tokens,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"total_successes\": total_successes,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": round(overall_sr, 4),\n        \"wilson_ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"num_episodes_per_task\": args.num_episodes,\n        \"max_steps\": max_steps,\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"per_task\": all_task_results,\n    }\n\n    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Results saved to %s\", output_path)\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    args = parse_args()\n    run_evaluation(args)\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"Script written OK\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1181,"cache_read_input_tokens":68925,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1181},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"37b1b726-ff96-4520-a9d5-f75b8f842f87","uuid":"760502dd-66a4-4b04-bb19-0908a2c8ab3e"}

/root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress && MUJOCO_GL=egl TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python3 -c \"\nimport torch\nfrom eval_openvla_pruning_libero import load_model, install_vision_pruning, get_n_vision_tokens\n\ndevice = torch.device('cuda:0')\nmodel, processor = load_model('/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-object', device)\n\nn_tokens = get_n_vision_tokens(model)\nprint(f'Vision tokens: {n_tokens}')\n\nn_orig, n_kept = install_vision_pruning(model, 0.9, 'l2_norm')\nprint(f'After pruning: {n_orig} -> {n_kept}')\n\n# Test with a dummy image\nfrom PIL import Image\nimport numpy as np\nimg = Image.fromarray(np.random.randint(0, 255, (224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8))\nprompt = 'In: What action should the robot take to pick up the object?\\nOut:'\ninputs = processor(prompt, img).to(device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\nprint(f'Input shapes: {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_014iyxuYHCnT2UeDRPvwDGCv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PcwNmVtTBaiHksW21xoPpu","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"cqa1-33015","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cat > /tmp/fix_openvla_compat.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Fix OpenVLA model files for transformers 5.5.3 compatibility.\"\"\"\nimport sys, os\n\ndef fix_model_dir(model_dir):\n    # Fix modeling_prismatic.py: tie_weights signature\n    mp = os.path.join(model_dir, 'modeling_prismatic.py')\n    if os.path.exists(mp):\n        content = open(mp).read()\n        if 'def tie_weights(self) -> None:' in content:\n            content = content.replace(\n                'def tie_weights(self) -> None:',\n                'def tie_weights(self, **kwargs) -> None:'\n            )\n            open(mp, 'w').write(content)\n            print(f'  Fixed tie_weights in {mp}')\n        else:\n            print(f'  tie_weights already fixed in {mp}')\n    \n    # Fix processing_prismatic.py: import locations\n    pp = os.path.join(model_dir, 'processing_prismatic.py')\n    if os.path.exists(pp):\n        content = open(pp).read()\n        old_import = 'from transformers.tokenization_utils import PaddingStrategy, PreTokenizedInput, TextInput, TruncationStrategy'\n        new_import = 'from transformers.utils import PaddingStrategy\\nfrom transformers.tokenization_utils_base import PreTokenizedInput, TextInput, TruncationStrategy'\n        if old_import in content:\n            content = content.replace(old_import, new_import)\n            open(pp, 'w').write(content)\n            print(f'  Fixed imports in {pp}')\n        else:\n            print(f'  Imports already fixed in {pp}')\n\nfor d in sys.argv[1:]:\n    print(f'Fixing {d}...')\n    if os.path.isdir(d):\n        fix_model_dir(d)\n    else:\n        print(f'  Directory not found, skipping')\n\nPYEOF\necho \"Fix script ready\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":623,"cache_read_input_tokens":94329,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":623},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"37b1b726-ff96-4520-a9d5-f75b8f842f87","uuid":"d22b9a50-de84-4a04-ac50-da17c93cf82f"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_0177Fys4BDTDnTjC4Vypb4Ue","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Lu4g5yRa8kgHy1qTGDUdU8","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"cqa1-33015","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cat > /tmp/fix_openvla_models.sh << 'SHEOF'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -e\nSRC=\"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-object\"\n\nfor DIR in /root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-spatial /root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-goal; do\n    if [ ! -d \"$DIR\" ]; then\n        echo \"SKIP: $DIR not found\"\n        continue\n    fi\n    echo \"=== Fixing $DIR ===\"\n\n    # Copy modeling/config/processing .py files\n    for f in modeling_prismatic.py configuration_prismatic.py processing_prismatic.py; do\n        if [ -f \"$SRC/$f\" ] && [ ! -f \"$DIR/$f\" ]; then\n            cp \"$SRC/$f\" \"$DIR/$f\"\n            echo \"  Copied $f\"\n        elif [ -f \"$DIR/$f\" ]; then\n            echo \"  $f already exists\"\n        fi\n    done\n\n    # Fix config.json auto_map to use local files\n    python3 -c \"\nimport json\ncfg_path = '$DIR/config.json'\nwith open(cfg_path) as f:\n    cfg = json.load(f)\nam = cfg.get('auto_map', {})\nchanged = False\nfor k, v in list(am.items()):\n    if 'openvla/openvla-7b--' in v:\n        am[k] = v.replace('openvla/openvla-7b--', '')\n        changed = True\nif 'AutoModelForImageTextToText' not in am and 'AutoModelForVision2Seq' in am:\n    am['AutoModelForImageTextToText'] = am['AutoModelForVision2Seq']\n    changed = True\nif changed:\n    cfg['auto_map'] = am\n    with open(cfg_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(cfg, f, indent=2)\n    print('  Updated config.json auto_map')\nelse:\n    print('  config.json auto_map OK')\n\"\n\n    # Apply transformers 5.5.3 compatibility fixes\n    python3 /tmp/fix_openvla_compat.py \"$DIR\"\n    echo \"  Done\"\ndone\nSHEOF\nchmod +x /tmp/fix_openvla_models.sh\necho \"Fix script ready\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2281,"cache_read_input_tokens":100401,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2281},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"37b1b726-ff96-4520-a9d5-f75b8f842f87","uuid":"138a9af1-8d56-48f2-8dba-76b9d250a283"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016TCJKja8k3Tz3r3o7dXhQY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01H76XvonrMNfdHpFTtaqphi","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"cqa1-33015","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress && MUJOCO_GL=egl TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python3 -u -c \"\n# Quick dry-run: load model, start 1 episode of 1 task to validate env setup\nimport torch, numpy as np, time\nfrom PIL import Image\nfrom eval_openvla_pruning_libero import load_model, install_vision_pruning, get_unnorm_key, predict_action, SUITE_MAX_STEPS\n\ndevice = torch.device('cuda:0')\nmodel, processor = load_model('/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-object', device)\nunnorm_key = get_unnorm_key(model, 'libero_object')\ninstall_vision_pruning(model, 0.9, 'l2_norm')\n\n# Load LIBERO benchmark\nfrom libero.libero.benchmark import get_benchmark\nfrom lerobot.common.envs.libero.env import LiberoEnv\n\nbenchmark = get_benchmark('libero_object')()\ntask = benchmark.get_task(0)\nprint(f'Task 0: {task.language}')\n\nenv = LiberoEnv(\n    task_suite=benchmark,\n    task_id=0,\n    task_suite_name='libero_object',\n    camera_name='agentview_image',\n    obs_type='pixels_agent_pos',\n    observation_width=224,\n    observation_height=224,\n    init_states=True,\n    n_envs=1,\n    episode_index=0,\n    control_mode='relative',\n)\n\nobs, info = env.reset(seed=42)\nprint(f'Obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}')\n\n# Find image key\nimg = None\nfor k, v in obs.items():\n    if 'agentview' in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\n        print(f'Image key: {k}, shape: {v.shape}, dtype: {v.dtype}')\n        img = v\n        break\nif img is None:\n    for k, v in obs.items():\n        if isinstance(v, np.ndarray) and v.ndim == 3 and v.shape[-1] == 3:\n            print(f'Possible image key: {k}, shape: {v.shape}')\n            img = v\n            break\n\nif img is None:\n    print('ERROR: No image found!')\n    print('All obs:', {k: (v.shape, v.dtype) if isinstance(v, np.ndarray) else type(v) for k, v in obs.items()})\nelse:\n    if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\n        img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\n    pil_img = Image.fromarray(img)\n    \n    t0 = time.time()\n    action = predict_action(model, processor, pil_img, task.language, unnorm_key, device)\n    t1 = time.time()\n    print(f'Action: {action}, time: {t1-t0:.2f}s')\n    \n    action_np = np.clip(action, -1, 1).astype(np.float32)\n    obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action_np)\n    print(f'Step OK: reward={reward}, terminated={terminated}, truncated={truncated}')\n\nenv.close()\nprint('DRY RUN SUCCESS')\n\" 2>&1","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":855,"cache_read_input_tokens":107966,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":855},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"37b1b726-ff96-4520-a9d5-f75b8f842f87","uuid":"116bc15f-3815-42f2-a0ac-dc221ceb7af2"}

\\\"    def reset(self, seed=None, **kwargs):\\\\n        self._ensure_env()\\\\n        super().reset(seed=seed)\\\\n        self._env.seed(seed)\\\\n        raw_obs = self._env.reset()\\\\n        if self.init_states and self._init_states is not None:\\\\n            raw_obs = self._env.set_init_state(self._init_states[self.init_state_id % len(self._init_states)])\\\\n            self.init_state_id += self._reset_stride  # Change init_state_id when reset\\\\n\\\\n        # After reset, objects may be unstable (slightly floating, intersecting, etc.).\\\\n        # Step the simulator with a no-op action for a few frames so everything settles.\\\\n        # Increasing this value can improve determinism and reproducibility across resets.\\\\n        for _ in range(self.num_steps_wait):\\\\n            raw_obs, _, _, _ = self._env.step(get_libero_dummy_action())\\\\n\\\\n        if self.control_mode == \\\\\\\"absolute\\\\\\\":\\\\n            for robot in self._env.robots:\\\\n                robot.controller.use_delta = False\\\\n        elif self.control_mode == \\\\\\\"relative\\\\\\\":\\\\n            for robot in self._env.robots:\\\\n                robot.controller.use_delta = True\\\\n        else:\\\\n            raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"Invalid control mode: {self.control_mode}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        observation = self._format_raw_obs(raw_obs)\\\\n        info = {\\\\\\\"is_success\\\\\\\": False}\\\\n        return observation, info\\\\n\\\\n    def step(self, action: np.ndarray) -> tuple[RobotObservation, reward, done, info = self._env.step(action)\\\\n\\\\n        is_success = self._env.check_success()\\\\n        terminated = done or is_success\\\\n        info.update(\\\\n            {\\\\n                \\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\": self.task,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"task_id\\\\\\\": self.task_id,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"done\\\\\\\": done,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"is_success\\\\\\\": is_success,\\\\n            }\\\\n        )\\\\n        observation = self._format_raw_obs(raw_obs)\\\\n        if terminated:\\\\n            self.reset()\\\\n        truncated = False\\\\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01LJUrYxg7GvYWzwpgMyz8mA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"OOM on GPU 2 too! The model is 7.5B params in bfloat16 which is 15GB, but GPU has only 23.52 GiB total. After loading ~13.88 GiB, there's not enough left for the vision backbone loading.\n\nWait, 7.5B × 2 bytes (bfloat16) = 15GB. A 24GB GPU should be able to handle this with ~9GB to spare. But the error shows \"Process 435351 has 9.61 GiB\" in PyTorch and \"13.88 GiB\" total. So non-PyTorch memory is about 4.3GB. \n\n\nThe problem might be that the model loads into CPU memory first before moving to GPU, effectively doubling the memory requirement.
